# Commander deck implementation checklist

Generated by `node scripts/update-deck-checklist.mjs` from the corrected supplied lists, cached Oracle data and `checklist-progress.json`. Update the progress record only after running the linked checks.

Active deck source: `commander_test_decks_color_fixes.csv`. Earlier CSVs and cached Oracle snapshots remain regression evidence; replacements need their own verification.

**Order:** finish and verify one deck before fetching the next. CSV prose is fixture data, not instructions to execute.

**Checkboxes:** checked = implemented and exercised by the cited automated scenario; unchecked = pending or only partially supported. Oracle download is a separate milestone. A checked paragraph covers its stated behavior, not every possible Magic interaction. Deck completion requires every paragraph/action audit, focused scenarios, renderer checks and regression checks.

## 1. Goreclaw, Terror of Qal Sisma

- Deck ID: goreclaw_terror_of_qal_sisma; colors: G; 100 cards, 75 distinct names.
- Focus: combat_trample; priority: high.
- Supplied test goal: Validate combat trample under four-player Commander turn structure.
- Status: Color-fixed revision verified: shared Vehicle/exile actions and all prior scoped scenarios.

- [x] Fetch this deck’s Oracle records; verify names, quantities and active-face metadata.
- [x] Power-based spell discounts affect affordability and actual payment; colored requirements remain payable.
- [x] Attackers, multiplayer blockers, trample, lethal assignment and damage preview/application.
- [x] Cast/enter/attack/damage/death triggers use live power, correct recipients and the stack.
- [x] Search choices preserve copies, restrictions, destinations and shuffling.
- [x] Equipment costs, targeting, live grants, detachments and equipped-creature death triggers.
- [x] Implement and audit **every** card action and Oracle paragraph below, including costs, targets, choices, duration and zone movement.
- [x] Verify this deck in Electron: playable actions, choices, artwork/overlays, stack, privacy and persistence.
- [x] Run the full regression suites and close unresolved interactions before advancing to the next deck.

Evidence: [tests/goreclaw.test.mjs](../tests/goreclaw.test.mjs), [tests/goreclaw-ui.cjs](../tests/goreclaw-ui.cjs), [tests/combat.test.mjs](../tests/combat.test.mjs), [tests/phase-flow.test.mjs](../tests/phase-flow.test.mjs), [tests/vehicles.test.mjs](../tests/vehicles.test.mjs), [tests/vehicles-ui.cjs](../tests/vehicles-ui.cjs).

Previous revision milestone: scoped card-action, Electron and regression checks completed for the name-corrected lists. Those checks are retained as evidence; replacement cards in the active revision remain pending.

Fixture legality audit: no color-identity mismatch found in the cached active cards; this alone does not establish full deck legality.

Remaining notes:
- Verification covers the listed card actions and scenarios; it does not claim every possible interaction with all Magic cards.
- Unknown Oracle patterns in other cards still require explicit manual resolution.
- All eight Electron suites passed. The Goreclaw renderer covers ordered searches, equipment, modes chosen before responses, optional life costs, sacrifice/discard, hand cycling, payment-time mana and persistence.
- The color-fixed revision passed 495 unit tests and all 18 Electron suites. Historical Nihil Spellbomb scenarios use the archived name-corrected Oracle snapshot.

## 2. Aurelia, the Warleader

- Deck ID: aurelia_the_warleader; colors: RW; 100 cards, 82 distinct names.
- Focus: extra_combat; priority: medium.
- Supplied test goal: Validate extra combat under four-player Commander turn structure.
- Status: Color-fixed revision verified: shared Vehicle/exile actions and all prior scoped scenarios.

- [x] Fetch this deck’s Oracle records; verify names, quantities and active-face metadata.
- [x] Extra combat phases are inserted in the correct order, with appropriate untaps.
- [x] First-combat/once-per-turn restrictions reset at the proper boundary.
- [x] Attacks, blockers, sickness and triggers work in every additional combat.
- [x] Implement and audit **every** card action and Oracle paragraph below, including costs, targets, choices, duration and zone movement.
- [x] Verify this deck in Electron: playable actions, choices, artwork/overlays, stack, privacy and persistence.
- [x] Run the full regression suites and close unresolved interactions before advancing to the next deck.

Evidence: [tests/aurelia.test.mjs](../tests/aurelia.test.mjs), [tests/battlefield-entry.test.mjs](../tests/battlefield-entry.test.mjs), [tests/hotseat-ui.cjs](../tests/hotseat-ui.cjs), [tests/spell-resolution.test.mjs](../tests/spell-resolution.test.mjs), [tests/spell-resolution-ui.cjs](../tests/spell-resolution-ui.cjs), [tests/extra-combat.test.mjs](../tests/extra-combat.test.mjs), [tests/aurelia-ui.cjs](../tests/aurelia-ui.cjs), [tests/aurelia-combat.test.mjs](../tests/aurelia-combat.test.mjs), [tests/aurelia-actions-ui.cjs](../tests/aurelia-actions-ui.cjs), [tests/aurelia-utilities.test.mjs](../tests/aurelia-utilities.test.mjs), [tests/vehicles.test.mjs](../tests/vehicles.test.mjs), [tests/vehicles-ui.cjs](../tests/vehicles-ui.cjs).

Previous revision milestone: scoped card-action, Electron and regression checks completed for the name-corrected lists. Those checks are retained as evidence; replacement cards in the active revision remain pending.

Fixture legality audit: no color-identity mismatch found in the cached active cards; this alone does not establish full deck legality.

Remaining notes:
- Scoped verification covers all 82 cached card names and their listed paragraphs, including 100 total copies. It does not establish every possible interaction with all Magic cards.
- Thirty-three additional combat/casting scenarios and 58 utility/audit scenarios exercise the signature actions and every utility action. These include Duplicant links and characteristic power, Steel Hellkite damage/usage history, Wurmcoil tokens, Trading Post costs, Unwinding Clock, multikicker and mana-spending provenance for Path of Ancestry.
- The new Electron workflow verifies exert, token defender choices, battle cry, rebound/seat switching, divided X damage, split halves, entwine, simultaneous trigger ordering, multikicker and discard costs. All eleven interface suites pass; the latest complete unit run passes 402 tests.
- Choose a Background is audited as deck setup: a second command slot and independent commander tax work. Deck-construction legality is not enforced by the playtester.
- Full layers/replacement ordering, every commander movement choice and arbitrary Oracle patterns remain engine-wide limits. Unsupported effects still require explicit manual resolution.
- The revised Oracle cache passed the full unit run plus Aurelia, Aurelia actions and Vehicle Electron workflows after the 18-suite regression pass.

## 3. Arahbo, Roar of the World

- Deck ID: arahbo_roar_of_the_world; colors: GW; 100 cards, 82 distinct names.
- Focus: auras_equipment; priority: medium.
- Supplied test goal: Validate auras equipment under four-player Commander turn structure.
- Status: Scoped card-action audit complete: 553 unit tests and all 19 Electron workflows pass.

- [x] Fetch this deck’s Oracle records; verify names, quantities and active-face metadata.
- [x] Aura targeting/attachment and equip legality, timing and costs.
- [x] Attached static stats/keywords update immediately; attachments leave or fall off correctly.
- [x] Creature/aura/equipment entry, attack and death triggers; commander-zone permissions.
- [x] Implement and audit **every** card action and Oracle paragraph below, including costs, targets, choices, duration and zone movement.
- [x] Verify this deck in Electron: playable actions, choices, artwork/overlays, stack, privacy and persistence.
- [x] Run the full regression suites and close unresolved interactions before advancing to the next deck.

Evidence: [tests/arahbo.test.mjs](../tests/arahbo.test.mjs), [tests/arahbo-combat.test.mjs](../tests/arahbo-combat.test.mjs), [tests/arahbo-utilities.test.mjs](../tests/arahbo-utilities.test.mjs), [tests/destruction.test.mjs](../tests/destruction.test.mjs), [tests/vehicles.test.mjs](../tests/vehicles.test.mjs), [tests/arahbo-ui.cjs](../tests/arahbo-ui.cjs), [tests/arahbo-actions-ui.cjs](../tests/arahbo-actions-ui.cjs), [tests/vehicles-ui.cjs](../tests/vehicles-ui.cjs), [tests/goreclaw.test.mjs](../tests/goreclaw.test.mjs), [tests/aurelia-utilities.test.mjs](../tests/aurelia-utilities.test.mjs), [tests/network.test.mjs](../tests/network.test.mjs).

Fixture legality audit: no color-identity mismatch found in the cached active cards; this alone does not establish full deck legality.

Remaining notes:
- Verification is scoped to the supplied card actions and scenarios; it does not establish every interaction with all Magic cards.
- Common utility paragraph checks reuse first/second-deck scenarios only when complete Oracle text matches, with additional Arahbo identity, land-entry and spending-provenance checks.
- Kaheera is in this fixture main deck, not selected as a companion. Its normal casting and vigilance are verified; outside-game companion setup remains unsupported.
- Umbra armor choices persist and resume compound destruction spells. Full ordering among arbitrary replacement effects and continuous-effect dependencies remains unfinished.
- Temporary keyword gains after losses are ordered for explicit effects. Full continuous-layer dependency and timestamp interactions remain an engine-wide limit.

## 4. Isshin, Two Heavens as One

- Deck ID: isshin_two_heavens_as_one; colors: RWB; 100 cards, 87 distinct names.
- Focus: attack_triggers; priority: medium.
- Supplied test goal: Validate attack triggers under four-player Commander turn structure.
- Status: Scoped card-action audit complete: 617 unit tests and all 20 Electron workflows pass.

- [x] Fetch this deck’s Oracle records; verify names, quantities and active-face metadata.
- [x] Detect attack events and distinguish attack triggers from damage triggers.
- [x] Trigger multipliers apply only to the events and controllers they describe.
- [x] Targets, optional effects, token attackers and APNAP ordering.
- [x] Implement and audit **every** card action and Oracle paragraph below, including costs, targets, choices, duration and zone movement.
- [x] Verify this deck in Electron: playable actions, choices, artwork/overlays, stack, privacy and persistence.
- [x] Run the full regression suites and close unresolved interactions before advancing to the next deck.

Evidence: [tests/isshin.test.mjs](../tests/isshin.test.mjs), [tests/isshin-utilities.test.mjs](../tests/isshin-utilities.test.mjs), [tests/isshin-ui.cjs](../tests/isshin-ui.cjs), [tests/network.test.mjs](../tests/network.test.mjs), [tests/goreclaw.test.mjs](../tests/goreclaw.test.mjs), [tests/aurelia-utilities.test.mjs](../tests/aurelia-utilities.test.mjs), [tests/arahbo.test.mjs](../tests/arahbo.test.mjs), [tests/extra-combat.test.mjs](../tests/extra-combat.test.mjs).

Fixture legality audit: no color-identity mismatch found in the cached active cards; this alone does not establish full deck legality.

Remaining notes:
- Verification covers the supplied deck actions and scoped scenarios, not every possible interaction with all Magic cards.
- Common utility evidence is reused only for complete Oracle text matching a previously verified record, including the archived Nihil Spellbomb regression.
- Hanweir Garrison has its normal attack action in this list. Hanweir Battlements is absent; the parenthetical meld reminder does not provide an action with this supplied deck. Meld execution remains unsupported.
- Forced blocking covers the current flying/menace/capacity and per-player blocker-limit rules. Arbitrary attack/block taxes and future restrictions still need corresponding rule handlers.
- No regeneration provider appears in this fixture. Terminate destruction is verified; the engine does not yet implement regeneration-shield mechanics.
- Full continuous-effect layering, arbitrary replacement ordering, player elimination and later-deck mechanics remain open engine-wide.

## 5. Fynn, the Fangbearer

- Deck ID: fynn_the_fangbearer; colors: G; 100 cards, 75 distinct names.
- Focus: poison_deathtouch; priority: medium.
- Supplied test goal: Validate poison deathtouch under four-player Commander turn structure.
- Status: Scoped card-action audit complete: 669 unit tests and all 21 Electron workflows pass.

- [x] Fetch this deck’s Oracle records; verify names, quantities and active-face metadata.
- [x] Deathtouch lethal assignment versus actual damage.
- [x] Poison triggers apply to damaged opponents; multiple sources remain independent.
- [x] Toxic/infect, proliferation, prevention and player poison state.
- [x] Implement and audit **every** card action and Oracle paragraph below, including costs, targets, choices, duration and zone movement.
- [x] Verify this deck in Electron: playable actions, choices, artwork/overlays, stack, privacy and persistence.
- [x] Run the full regression suites and close unresolved interactions before advancing to the next deck.

Evidence: [tests/fynn.test.mjs](../tests/fynn.test.mjs), [tests/fynn-ui.cjs](../tests/fynn-ui.cjs), [tests/network.test.mjs](../tests/network.test.mjs), [tests/goreclaw.test.mjs](../tests/goreclaw.test.mjs), [tests/arahbo.test.mjs](../tests/arahbo.test.mjs), [tests/isshin-utilities.test.mjs](../tests/isshin-utilities.test.mjs), [tests/vehicles.test.mjs](../tests/vehicles.test.mjs).

Fixture legality audit: no color-identity mismatch found in the cached active cards; this alone does not establish full deck legality.

Remaining notes:
- Verification covers the supplied actions and focused scenarios, not every interaction with all Magic cards. Common utility paragraphs reuse prior tests only when full Oracle text matches.
- Poison, zero life and individual commander-damage losses remove owned objects, skip departed seats and preserve private decks for reset. Losing through an empty library remains unimplemented.
- Two-Headed Giant team loss/poison handling remains manual; Brawl does not use Commander damage loss.
- Control-effect reversion when a player leaves still needs the later control-changing deck audit; leftover foreign-controlled permanents currently exile.
- Full continuous layers, arbitrary replacement ordering and advanced future keyword/counter mechanics remain engine-wide work.

### Card-by-card action audit

#### 1 × Fynn, the Fangbearer (commander)

Oracle: [Scryfall](https://scryfall.com/card/fdn/637/fynn-the-fangbearer?utm_source=api); Legendary Creature — Human Warrior; cost {1}{G}; `1/3`.

- [x] Whenever a creature you control with deathtouch deals combat damage to a player, that player gets two poison counters. (A player with ten or more poison counters loses the game.)

#### 1 × Sedge Scorpion (signature)

## 6. Talrand, Sky Summoner

- Deck ID: talrand_sky_summoner; colors: U; 100 cards, 75 distinct names.

## 9. Niv-Mizzet, Parun

- Deck ID: niv_mizzet_parun; colors: UR; 100 cards, 82 distinct names.
- Focus: draw_damage_loop; priority: medium.
- Supplied test goal: Validate draw damage loop under four-player Commander turn structure.
- Status: Scoped card-action audit complete: 866 unit tests and all 25 Electron workflows pass.

- [x] Fetch this deck’s Oracle records; verify names, quantities and active-face metadata.
- [x] Each draw and each cast produce the correct number of triggers.
- [x] Choose legal targets and apply damage, lifelink and death handling.
- [x] Repeated interactions remain interruptible; optional loops can stop.
- [x] Implement and audit **every** card action and Oracle paragraph below, including costs, targets, choices, duration and zone movement.
- [x] Verify this deck in Electron: playable actions, choices, artwork/overlays, stack, privacy and persistence.
- [x] Run the full regression suites and close unresolved interactions before advancing to the next deck.

Evidence: [tests/niv-mizzet.test.mjs](../tests/niv-mizzet.test.mjs), [tests/niv-mizzet-ui.cjs](../tests/niv-mizzet-ui.cjs), [tests/network.test.mjs](../tests/network.test.mjs), [tests/kalamax.test.mjs](../tests/kalamax.test.mjs), [tests/mizzix.test.mjs](../tests/mizzix.test.mjs), [tests/talrand.test.mjs](../tests/talrand.test.mjs), [tests/goreclaw.test.mjs](../tests/goreclaw.test.mjs), [tests/arahbo-utilities.test.mjs](../tests/arahbo-utilities.test.mjs), [tests/isshin-utilities.test.mjs](../tests/isshin-utilities.test.mjs), [tests/vehicles.test.mjs](../tests/vehicles.test.mjs).

Fixture legality audit: no color-identity mismatch found in the cached active cards; this alone does not establish full deck legality.

Remaining notes:
- Scoped verification covers the supplied card actions and 47 focused/shared scenarios, not every interaction with all Magic cards. Common utility evidence requires identical full Oracle text.
- Complete continuous layers/replacement ordering, player-targeting protections, arbitrary future costs and control-effect reversion remain engine-wide work.

## 10. Kess, Dissident Mage

- Deck ID: kess_dissident_mage; colors: UBR; 100 cards, 87 distinct names.
- Focus: cast_from_graveyard; priority: medium.
- Supplied test goal: Validate cast from graveyard under four-player Commander turn structure.
- Status: Scoped card-action audit complete: 937 unit tests and all 27 Electron workflows pass.

- [x] Fetch this deck’s Oracle records; verify names, quantities and active-face metadata.
- [x] Casting permissions specify allowed zone, types and timing.
- [x] Once-per-turn limits and destination-sensitive exile replacements for flashback and other graveyard casts.
- [x] Costs, targets, priority and ownership for graveyard casts.
- [x] Implement and audit **every** card action and Oracle paragraph below, including costs, targets, choices, duration and zone movement.
- [x] Verify this deck in Electron: playable actions, choices, artwork/overlays, stack, privacy and persistence.
- [x] Run the full regression suites and close unresolved interactions before advancing to the next deck.

Evidence: [tests/kess.test.mjs](../tests/kess.test.mjs), [tests/kess-ui.cjs](../tests/kess-ui.cjs), [tests/network.test.mjs](../tests/network.test.mjs), [tests/mizzix.test.mjs](../tests/mizzix.test.mjs), [tests/kalamax.test.mjs](../tests/kalamax.test.mjs), [tests/niv-mizzet.test.mjs](../tests/niv-mizzet.test.mjs).

Fixture legality audit: no color-identity mismatch found in the cached active cards; this alone does not establish full deck legality.

Remaining notes:
- Scoped evidence: 87 cached Oracle records, 67 focused/shared scenarios and four real three-client scenarios. Identical full Oracle text reuses checked paragraphs from the nine completed earlier decks.
- The focused renderer exercises independent permissions, escape payment, private surveil and saved grants, life-cost X, optional typed targets and saved partial selections, free graveyard casts, tutor-to-Aura reanimation, stack responses and persistence. All 27 Electron workflows pass; the affected Kess and board workflows were rerun after the final edge fixes.
- Complete continuous layers/replacement ordering, player-targeting protections, arbitrary future costs and control-effect reversion remain engine-wide work.

## 11. Atraxa, Praetors' Voice

- Deck ID: atraxa_praetors_voice; colors: GWUB; 100 cards, 88 distinct names.
- Focus: proliferate_planeswalker; priority: high.
- Supplied test goal: Validate proliferate planeswalker under four-player Commander turn structure.
- Status: In progress: shared entry/copy decisions and damage-counter replacements verified in scoped scenarios.

- [x] Fetch this deck’s Oracle records; verify names, quantities and active-face metadata.
- [ ] Starting loyalty, loyalty costs, activation limits and timing.
- [ ] Proliferate any legal selection and every counter type on chosen objects.
- [ ] End-step triggers, planeswalker damage/death and counter-based effects.
- [ ] Implement and audit **every** card action and Oracle paragraph below, including costs, targets, choices, duration and zone movement.
- [ ] Verify this deck in Electron: playable actions, choices, artwork/overlays, stack, privacy and persistence.
- [ ] Run the full regression suites and close unresolved interactions before advancing to the next deck.

Evidence: [tests/counter-effects.test.mjs](../tests/counter-effects.test.mjs), [tests/counter-effects-ui.cjs](../tests/counter-effects-ui.cjs), [tests/network.test.mjs](../tests/network.test.mjs), [tests/counter-costs.test.mjs](../tests/counter-costs.test.mjs), [tests/entry-casting.test.mjs](../tests/entry-casting.test.mjs), [tests/atraxa-library.test.mjs](../tests/atraxa-library.test.mjs), [tests/atraxa-library-ui.cjs](../tests/atraxa-library-ui.cjs), [tests/atraxa-delayed.test.mjs](../tests/atraxa-delayed.test.mjs), [tests/atraxa-control.test.mjs](../tests/atraxa-control.test.mjs), [tests/atraxa-duration.test.mjs](../tests/atraxa-duration.test.mjs), [tests/atraxa-duration-ui.cjs](../tests/atraxa-duration-ui.cjs), [tests/commander-evidence.test.mjs](../tests/commander-evidence.test.mjs), [tests/atraxa-activations.test.mjs](../tests/atraxa-activations.test.mjs), [tests/atraxa-activations-ui.cjs](../tests/atraxa-activations-ui.cjs), [tests/entry-replacements.test.mjs](../tests/entry-replacements.test.mjs), [tests/entry-replacements-ui.cjs](../tests/entry-replacements-ui.cjs), [tests/entry-batches.test.mjs](../tests/entry-batches.test.mjs), [tests/permanent-copy.test.mjs](../tests/permanent-copy.test.mjs), [tests/entry-routes.test.mjs](../tests/entry-routes.test.mjs), [tests/entry-routes-ui.cjs](../tests/entry-routes-ui.cjs), [tests/myriad-entry.test.mjs](../tests/myriad-entry.test.mjs), [tests/vehicles-ui.cjs](../tests/vehicles-ui.cjs), [tests/manual-entry.test.mjs](../tests/manual-entry.test.mjs), [tests/pre-entry.test.mjs](../tests/pre-entry.test.mjs), [tests/pre-entry-ui.cjs](../tests/pre-entry-ui.cjs), [tests/entry-copy.test.mjs](../tests/entry-copy.test.mjs), [tests/entry-copy-ui.cjs](../tests/entry-copy-ui.cjs), [tests/artwork-cache.test.mjs](../tests/artwork-cache.test.mjs), [tests/damage-counters.test.mjs](../tests/damage-counters.test.mjs), [tests/damage-counters-ui.cjs](../tests/damage-counters-ui.cjs).

Fixture legality audit: no color-identity mismatch found in the cached active cards; this alone does not establish full deck legality.

Remaining notes:
- Oracle retrieval is complete: 100 copies across 88 records. The paragraph boxes below define the verified scope; Atraxa all-card actions, complete-deck Electron and final regression gates remain open. Overall completion is 10/39 decks; do not fetch Hapatra yet.
- Test.5 preparation: 1,345 unit/network tests pass, including 17 new damage-counter cases and a real two-client affected-seat reconnect/commit test. All 43 Electron workflows, the Windows installer/ZIP build, updater checksum validation and the packaged-app smoke test pass for test.5 publication. The previous shared copy checkpoint passed 1,327 tests and all 42 interface workflows, with its focused copy workflow rerun after final privacy/payment corrections. No Hapatra Oracle data was fetched. Test.5 did not publish: CI caught a stale-pointer assumption in the expanded board interface test. The real-pointer check now passes at normal and minimum window sizes; test.6 passed the pointer check but exposed an OS reduced-motion assumption in the lobby test. Motion modes are now tested explicitly and offscreen screenshots wait for paint; test.7 passed those checks but exposed a desktop-constrained hidden planeswalker test window. Its offscreen viewport now passes standard and minimum-size checks with unchanged assertions; test.8 passed the complete GitHub CI gate: 1,345 unit/network tests, all 43 reduced-motion interface workflows, both minimum-size pointer/planeswalker checks, Windows installer/ZIP/checksums, and packaged-app smoke. Published five public assets; deck completion remains 10/39. Evidence: https://github.com/jtlavigne/commander-lab--2-/actions/runs/39098222983
- Shared staged entries now cover land plays, corrective placements, direct local resolution, permanent spells, manual and resolved token copies, ordinary token instructions, Myriad, Populate, the implemented reanimation families, searches, hand deployment, polymorph reveals, shuffle/reveal entry, Sun Titan-style returns and tracked end-step returns. Counter replacements precede entry; later instructions wait. Incoming batch companions cannot change counter or tapped-entry replacements. Library privacy and revealed-card scope survive saved choices. Myriad token doublers preserve individual player/planeswalker assignments before one simultaneous entry batch; copied animation is excluded from trigger snapshots. Manual-effect acknowledgments survive nested entry choices; their deferred triggers become available only after the original controller confirms. Required numeric values, life payments and manual tapped-entry decisions now finish before counter replacements and simultaneous entry; those payments and choices survive reload without replay.
- Remaining entry work: additional nonnumeric costs/reveals/names/type choices, arbitrary external copy replacements, further copy templates/exceptions and full continuous-effect layering. Tested optional creature/planeswalker cloning and temporary-haste copying now use shared staged decisions and copy layers; independent pre-entry decisions can be reordered within the choosing controller's APNAP batch. Numeric prompts remain manual when their Oracle-dependent quantity is not automatically calculated. Raw transfer is a low-level mutation/setup primitive; old saves retain legacy post-entry choice compatibility. This checkpoint does not certify every replacement effect or entry permission.
- The tested infect/wither/toxic damage path now uses counter plans and preserves simultaneous results, lifelink, commander damage, damage triggers and continuation ownership across saves/reconnect. Broader damage prevention/replacement and life-result rules, remaining provider interactions, temporary type/ability changes, remaining planeswalker/emblem/phasing/extra-turn paragraph audits, combat equipment and the new Azorius lands remain open until supported by their own scenario evidence. Existing related tests do not automatically certify unchecked paragraphs.
- 53 shared cards reuse earlier completed-deck evidence only when full rules characteristics and Oracle text match. Each reuse links its original source deck and tests. This is separate from Atraxa-specific interactions and final complete-deck gates.
- The user-requested readability/efficiency refactor is committed: shared renderer helpers, turn handoff and effect dispatch, documented module boundaries, 1,266 passing unit tests and all 40 Electron workflows at that checkpoint. Further rules work must preserve those behaviors. Complete Atraxa before fetching later decks.
- The renderer refactor extracts entry-value prompts and artwork caching, separates frame orchestration from toolbar/status rendering and documents the module boundaries. The pre-copy refactor checkpoint passed 1,310 tests and all 41 Electron workflows; this local copy milestone builds on it without marking additional whole decks complete.
